# Gap closure, 2026-10-14

Date: 2026-10-14. Full outbound network access was granted today, so Hugging Face (the model API, file trees, raw cards, licences and config files), arXiv, the Unsloth docs, Artificial Analysis, LMArena's own data export, LiveBench, Vals, the vendor sites (AMD, Framework, HP, GMKtec, Minisforum, NVIDIA, Google, z.ai) and GitHub were read directly rather than through search snippets. This note closes the gaps listed in the report's "What a follow-up must close" section and records every statement of the report that the primary evidence contradicts.

Scope: 276 gaps across eight topics; 265 primary, 1 excerpt, 10 unresolved. Status meanings: primary = the page, API response or file was opened and read; excerpt = the page could not be opened and a search snippet was relied on; unresolved = no source resolves the gap (what would close it is listed under "Still open"). Verification = the verdict of the verification pass, recorded as returned ("no verdict" for every entry). Sizes are given as the collectors computed them: decimal GB = bytes / 1e9, GiB = bytes / 2^30, with shard counts; where two sources disagree both are recorded. The Value column carries the collector's note after "Note:". Values, units, dates and URLs are reproduced exactly as returned; only pipe characters and line breaks inside cells were escaped for the tables.

Writer's reconciliations (arithmetic on the recorded values, not new evidence): (1) the quant-tables Qwen3.8-Flash-Next totals "Q8_0 7 shards 192,362,462,368 B" and "BF16 9 shards 354,937,473,440 B" equal the sizes-core totals plus the MTP head and mmproj files that sit beside them in the same repository (188,225,033,248 + 4,137,429,120 = 192,362,462,368; 354,029,930,496 + 907,542,944 = 354,937,473,440), so the model-only figures are the sizes-core ones; (2) likewise the quant-tables Qwen3.8-27B "BF16 3 shards 55,588,882,048 B" equals the sizes-core BF16 54,657,735,616 B plus the 931,146,432 B mmproj-BF16; (3) Artificial Analysis' "180B total" for Qwen3.8-Flash-Next equals the card's 125B + 51B n-gram embedding + 4B MTP; (4) the report's Qwen3.5-122B-A10B "UD-Q6_K_XL 106 GiB" and "UD-Q4_K_XL 78.6 GB" match the unsloth MTP-repository files (113,872,342,144 B = 106.05 GiB; 78,646,479,904 B = 78.65 GB), while the plain repository's files are 112,401,249,920 B = 104.68 GiB and 77,029,996,032 B = 77.03 GB; (5) the Gemma 4 licence page was read in the cards-licences pass (Apache 2.0 text) even though the sizes-companions note records it as unread.

Node-side items from the report's follow-up list that remain open after this pass (nothing external can close them): the real KV footprints the kv-estimates to-do promises and whether UD-Q8_K_XL plus a q8_0 cache fits at 16K to 32K; Qwen3.5-122B document quality with the mmproj (the "without DeepStack" qualifier is dropped, since the released config has deepstack_visual_indexes = []); Qwen3.8-27B against Gemma 4 31B on the Principal's own scans and drawings; GLM-OCR throughput on gfx1151; Qwen3-ASR word-error rate on the Principal's audio; Qwen3-TTS output on the pinned Vulkan build through llama-tts (no PR comment confirms Vulkan output after #28253); Chatterbox Turbo and the CTranslate2 ROCm wheel on the ROCm 10.0 image; validation of the 192 GB GTT and TTM line (AMD documents ttm.pages_limit, the one published 192 GB profile is cafonez's ttm.pages_limit=46137344, and the node's amdgpu.gttsize=196608 remains validated only by V3).

Items that are closed as to evidence but carry a caveat the node must test: the two Nemotron Puzzle Q8_0 GGUFs (RemySkye, YanissAmz) were converted on a pre-merge PR #25444 commit, so loadability on the merged v0.4.1 mainline is unverified; the March 2026 mradermacher Heretic GGUFs of Nemotron 3 Super may lack the mtp.layers.* tensors the uploader's re-upload restored on 2026-05-02; the Voodisss Qwen3-Reranker GGUF headers were not inspected (only the mradermacher 8B header was, and it lacks the rank head), so the 4B and 8B rerankers should be converted locally with the pinned converter or their headers checked before use; the rerank input limit at the pin (query plus document must fit -ub) stands until a post-pin build.

Genuinely unavailable within the pin (only a newer llama.cpp closes them): MiMo-V2.6 (#29257, 22 September), GLM-5.3-Flash (#27773, 30 September) and its MTP (#29928), Nemotron 3 Super MTP (#29018, 17 September), Qwen3.8-Flash-Next MTP (#29761, 1 October) and its Vulkan hyper-connection ops (#28988), MiniCPM-V 4.7 (#29416), the rerank batch fix (#28876), a llama-server TTS endpoint (#26603 still open on master), and an upstream Vulkan MUL_MAT_ID F32 fix for Mistral Small 4 (none exists).

Secondary-only facts not worth a node run: the HP ZBook Ultra G3a ship month (October, snippets only) and the Micro Center Ryzen AI Halo price ($7,299.99, excerpt); the cause of the 25.6 GB difference between the unsloth (188.23 GB) and ggml-org (162.62 GB) Qwen3.8-Flash-Next Q8_0 builds, which repository metadata does not explain; and the MedGemma parameter counts behind the HAI-DEF gate, which closes once the Principal accepts the gate with their own account.

## Report statements corrected

The collectors' corrections, one bullet per report (or note) statement, each quoting the statement and giving the correction with the verified value. Line numbers refer to the report as it stood before the addendum.

### sizes-core

- Fit table, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next row: "Q6/Q8 class ≈103 to 133 GB plus table; Q8 unverified" is wrong. The table is inside the GGUF and the totals already include it: unsloth Q8_0 is 188,225,033,248 bytes = 188.23 GB = 175.30 GiB (does NOT fit 164 GiB; bartowski Q8_0 188.26 GB); unsloth UD-Q6_K_XL 169.17 GB = 157.55 GiB and bartowski Q6_K 168.11 GB = 156.57 GiB fit with only ~6.5 to 7.5 GiB left before KV. The "no at Q8" co-residency verdict stands; "yes at IQ4" (93.68 GB) stands.
- Decode section: "Qwen3.8-Flash-Next ... 41.3 tok/s with MTP on Vulkan" and the general claim that native MTP is a free speed-up on the MoE engines: for Flash-Next the unsloth MTP card states "A stock ggml-org/llama.cpp build cannot use these. Mainline has no MTP graph for the qwen4exp architecture ... no --spec-type draft-mtp option"; it needs the unsloth fork (b10715-mix-86bd2d3+) or unmerged PR #28243, so the pinned v0.4.1 cannot get the MTP figure. (Qwen3.5-122B MTP via PR #22673, merged 16 May 2026, is inside the pin.)
- Fit table, Qwen3.5-397B-A17B: "UD-IQ3_XXS 140.3 GB ... 3-bit only" is confirmed for IQ3_XXS (140.33 GB = 130.70 GiB), but not every 3-bit rung fits: UD-Q3_K_XL is 178.74 GB = 166.46 GiB (exceeds 164 GiB); the largest fitting rungs are UD-Q3_K_S 164.32 GB = 153.04 GiB and UD-IQ3_S 146.37 GB = 136.32 GiB.
- Fit table, Hy3: "Q4_K_M ≈177 GB; Q3 ≈135 GB ... 3-bit only" is wrong on the verdict. Q4_K_M is 182.33 GB (AngelSlim) / 182.16 GB (bartowski) = 169.7 GiB (no fit), but bartowski IQ4_XS is 160.77 GB = 149.73 GiB and YanissAmz Hy3-IQ4-UD-split is 143.87 GB = 133.99 GiB, so Hy3 fits at a 4.25-bpw rung; Q3_K_M is 134.60 GB (AngelSlim) / 137.55 GB (bartowski). Tencent's own AngelSlim GGUFs exist (apache-2.0) and need only PR #25395 (merged 2026-07-14, inside the pin).
- Fit table, Step-3.7-Flash: "Q6_K ≈162 GB estimate; Q6 borderline" - the official repo has no Q6_K (rungs BF16/Q8_0/Q4_K_S/IQ4_XS/Q3_K_L/Q3_K_M/IQ3_XXS); unsloth UD-Q6_K is 163.34 GB = 152.12 GiB (fits), unsloth UD-Q6_K_XL 178.70 GB = 166.43 GiB (does not), bartowski Q6_K 171.76 GB = 159.96 GiB (fits weights-only with ~4 GiB spare); official Q8_0 is 209.42 GB = 195.04 GiB. Q4_K_S 111.50 GB confirmed.
- Fit table, MiMo-V2.6-Flash: "≈170 to 175 GB estimate; borderline, likely no" - the native MXFP4 pass-through is 167.37 GB = 155.87 GiB (ggml-org, RL checkpoint; fits weights-only with ~8 GiB spare) or 174.91 GB = 162.90 GiB (MOPD checkpoint); there is no lower 4-bit rung because the experts are native MXFP4; the model remains outside the pin.
- Fit table, Mistral Medium 3.5: "Q8_0 ≈136 GB estimate" -> 132.85 GB = 123.73 GiB (unsloth and bartowski identical); Q6_K 102.58 GB (unsloth) / 107.80 GB (bartowski). Mistral Small 4: Q8_0 is 126.47 GB = 117.79 GiB; the "98 GB q8_0 with FFN q6_K" figure corresponds to unsloth UD-Q6_K at 99.43 GB.
- Fit table and co-residency arithmetic, Nemotron 3 Super: "Q8_0 120 to 123 GB" is 128.47 GB = 119.65 GiB (unsloth and bartowski), i.e. the DOC's figure is GiB-like; the "186 GB pair" with gpt-oss-120b (63.39 GB) is 191.86 GB = 178.68 GiB, which still does not fit. UD-IQ4_XS 64.48 GB (unsloth) but its folder is byte-identical to UD-IQ4_NL.
- Fit table, Nemotron 3 Puzzle: "Q8_0 ≈80 GB estimate" -> 83.45 GB = 77.72 GiB (RemySkye and YanissAmz); the pair with gpt-oss is 146.84 GB = 136.8 GiB plus caches. Both GGUFs were converted on a pre-merge PR #25444 commit; YanissAmz measured Q8_0 at 10.2 tok/s on Strix Halo Vulkan.
- DeepSeek V4 Flash 0731 row: the sizes 155.1 GB / 161.9 GB are confirmed as primary (unsloth/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731-GGUF UD-Q4_K_XL 155,095,241,120 bytes, UD-Q8_K_XL 161,869,615,520 bytes) and the Unsloth docs table (read in full) attributes them to the 0731 guide with 'all 1,328 are bit-identical' and 'UD-Q4_K_XL 155.1 ... 0.0102 ... 96.28%'. Caveat: the repo unsloth/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-GGUF holds the PREVIEW checkpoint (base_model deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V4-Flash), so the node must pull the -0731- repo id; no UD-Q6_K_XL rung exists for either.
- DeepSeek V4.1 Flash "smallest usable GGUF is 246 GiB" stands (vcruz305 Q2_K 264.52 GB = 246.35 GiB, "Q2_K is the floor", not runnable on upstream llama.cpp); the apetersson MixedQ2 at 157.33 GiB is smaller but excludes the 202.75 GB Engram tables and has no working runtime, so it does not change the verdict. No unsloth V4.1 GGUF exists.
- Fit table, Qwen3.5-122B-A10B: "Q8_0 130 GB; UD-Q6_K_XL 106 GiB; UD-Q4_K_XL 78.6 GB" - Q8_0 is 129.87 GB = 120.95 GiB (confirmed); the 106 GiB and 78.6 GB figures are the unsloth/Qwen3.5-122B-A10B-MTP-GGUF files (113.87 GB = 106.05 GiB; 78.65 GB), while the plain repo's are 112.40 GB = 104.68 GiB and 77.03 GB. No Qwen-official GGUF exists.
- GLM-5.3-Flash row: 147.5 GB / 199.7 GB and the 143.5 GiB Gorgon build are confirmed as primary (147,535,922,080 / 199,707,321,472 / 154,119,930,880 bytes); the Gorgon card was measured on the exact 'AMD Ryzen AI Max+ PRO 495 ... 192 GB' silicon on Vulkan (17.4 tok/s decode, 256.4 tok/s prefill, 15.0 tok/s at 32K) but requires post-#27773 llama.cpp, so the 'excerpt' label can be dropped and the pin caveat kept. No plain Q6_K exists; Q8_0 is 340.98 GB.
- gpt-oss-120b 63 GB and Qwen3.8-27B (Q8_0 ≈29 GB, BF16 55.6 GB) are confirmed: 63,387,346,208 bytes; 29.05 GB (unsloth) / 28.60 GB (ggml-org); BF16 safetensors 55.56 GB, BF16 GGUF 54.66 GB.

### sizes-companions

- Report (Companions, medicine): "served via SGLang or vLLM with an official GPTQ-INT4 and no GGUF mentioned" and "Its base is the qwen3moe architecture llama.cpp supports, so a community GGUF is plausible but unverified. If none exists, MedGemma-27B ... is the cheap co-resident alternative." Contradicted: Baichuan publishes an official GGUF, baichuan-inc/Baichuan-M3-235B-Q4_K_M-GGUF (10 shards, 143,782,829,376 B = 143.783 GB = 133.908 GiB, lastModified 2026-02-09, apache-2.0, card: "you can use llama.cpp or ollama"), plus an official FP8 repo (236.4 GB); community GGUFs exist from mradermacher (static and i1, Q2_K to Q6_K), DevQuasar (Q2_K to Q8_0) and spicyneuron (UD-Q4_K_XL 134.1 GB). The report's "roughly 130 to 144 GB ... fits only at Q4" should read: official Q4_K_M 133.9 GiB; mradermacher Q5_K_S 150.8 GiB and Q5_K_M 155.4 GiB also sit under 164 GiB on file size alone; Q6_K (179.8 GiB) and Q8_0 (232.8 GiB) do not.
- Report (Abliterated): "Nemotron 3 Super has no working abliteration at all: the one 'uncensored' GGUF found 'refuses ...'". Contradicted in part: Heretic builds exist, trohrbaugh/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16-heretic (Heretic v1.2.0, card: KL divergence 0.1081, refusals 28/100 vs 99/100) and -heretic-v2 (ARA, KL 0.2127, refusals 11/100 vs 100/100), with mradermacher GGUFs at Q8_0 128.473 GB (119.650 GiB) and Q6_K 112.922 GB (105.167 GiB) and a Sabomako v2 BF16 GGUF; the numbers are the uploader's own and no independent test was found, and the timteh673 file's two "not uncensored" discussions remain open. The Heretic README's "pure state-space models ... not yet supported" does not exclude this Mamba-2/MoE/attention hybrid.
- Report (Abliterated): "other uploaders list abliterations of the same base at Q6_K 100.4 GB and Q8_0 121 to 130 GB". Correction: every Q8_0 of an abliterated Qwen3.5-122B-A10B (mradermacher Chompa1422 build, wangzhang v2, Thaurock, mradermacher v1, heretic, heretic-v2, Sabomako) is 129,871,934,xxx B = 129.872 GB = 120.953 GiB, so "121" and "130" are one file size in GiB and GB; Q6_K is 100,307,731,xxx B = 100.308 GB = 93.419 GiB. The only larger Q8_0 is llmfan46's MTP-preserved build at 135.784 GB (126.458 GiB). huihui's own repo still holds only Q4_K (74.208 GB) plus an f16 mmproj.
- Report (Abliterated): "huihui-ai is the only producer with a near-complete GGUF line across every candidate family (gpt-oss-120b, ...)". Contradicted for gpt-oss-120b: huihui-ai never published a 120b GGUF repo (the GGUFs are mradermacher's split-part files and noctrex's MXFP4_MOE), and on 2026-10-14 huihui-ai's three gpt-oss-120b source repos (BF16-abliterated, BF16-abliterated-v2, mxfp4-abliterated) return HTTP 401 and are absent from the author listing, so the provenance of the node's noctrex file (base: BF16-abliterated-v2) can no longer be re-read; the noctrex file itself is still public at 63,387,346,976 B = 63.387 GB = 59.034 GiB.
- Report (Vision): Gemma 4 31B has "reported Apache 2.0 licensing, but its Gemma Terms history ... needs the official card to confirm the licence change". Closed: the official google/gemma-4-31B-it card states license: apache-2.0 with license_link https://ai.google.dev/gemma/docs/gemma_4_license and the repo is not gated; the linked licence page itself was not read.
- Report (Core seats / follow-up): Qwen3.8-Flash-Next's "licence ... still being written down" and the notes' "unverified" licence. Closed and not Apache: Qwen/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next ships license: other, license_name qwen-community-1.0; the LICENSE file is the "Qwen Community License 1.0", which requires a separate licence from Qwen for any "Model as a Service or AI Work Assistant business" (internal use exempt) and model-name display above 100,000,000 MAU or US$20,000,000 monthly revenue. Qwen3.8-27B is confirmed apache-2.0.
- Report (follow-up section): "no GGUF file size was read from a repository for Qwen3.5-122B Q8_0, Nemotron 3 Super Q8_0, Qwen3-Coder-Next, Baichuan-M3, Gemma 4 31B Q8_0 or any Qwen3.8-Flash-Next rung above IQ4_XS". All now read from the HF API: Qwen3.5-122B Q8_0 129.872 GB (120.953 GiB, MTP build 132.554 GB); Nemotron 3 Super Q8_0 128.473 GB (119.650 GiB); Qwen3-Coder-Next official Q8_0 84.812 GB (78.987 GiB) and Q6_K 65.528 GB (61.028 GiB); Baichuan-M3 official Q4_K_M 143.783 GB (133.908 GiB); Gemma 4 31B Q8_0 32.636 GB (30.394 GiB); Qwen3.8-Flash-Next UD-Q4_K_XL 111.335 GB (103.688 GiB), UD-Q5_K_XL 158.286 GB (147.416 GiB), UD-Q6_K_XL 169.165 GB (157.548 GiB), Q8_0 188.225 GB unsloth / 162.625 GB ggml-org (the two Q8_0 builds disagree by 25.6 GB; both recorded).
- Report (Companions, retrieval): "confirmation that the llama-server embedding and reranking endpoints accept these architectures, which the notes do not establish". Closed: Qwen's own Qwen3-Embedding GGUF card gives "llama-server -m model.gguf --embedding --pooling last"; there is no official Qwen3-Reranker GGUF, and the only verified-working conversions (Voodisss/Qwen3-Reranker-{4B,8B}-GGUF-llama_cpp, created 2026-03-09) run with "llama-server --reranking --pooling rank --embedding" on /v1/rerank, after llama.cpp issue #16407 (closed 2025-10-09) attributed wrong scores to badly converted GGUFs lacking cls.output.weight / pooling RANK; mradermacher and QuantFactory reranker Q8_0 files are ~660 MB larger and their scoring is not verified.
- Report (Speech): "Qwen3-TTS 0.6B/1.7B ... gained llama.cpp support on 4 August 2026". Nuance: the only ggml-org GGUF is Qwen3-TTS-12Hz-1.7B-Base (lastModified 2026-08-03, no README); no ggml-org 0.6B, CustomVoice or VoiceDesign GGUF exists, and the available 0.6B GGUFs (Serveurperso qwentts.cpp, Volko76 Qwen3tts.cpp, lainlives) are separate-runtime formats not verified for llama.cpp.
- Report (Abliterated): "huihui's DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 abliteration added a Q4_K on 3 August 2026". Confirmed (repo lastModified 2026-08-03), with sizes the report lacked: Q4_K 164.634 GB = 153.327 GiB and Q4-mxfp4 155.976 GB = 145.264 GiB, so the Q4_K rung leaves about 11 GiB of the 164 GiB budget before any KV cache; the card also states the GGUFs come from antirez/deepseek-v4-gguf and that "all expert modules were not ablated".
- Notes (abliterated_variants.md): huihui Qwen3.8-Flash-Next abliteration described without sizes; the huihui repo's folders are byte-for-byte unsloth's base GGUFs (Q8_0 188,225,033,440 vs 188,225,033,248 B; card: "GGUFs come from unsloth/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-GGUF"), so no rung between UD-Q4_K_XL (103.7 GiB) and Q8_0 (175.3 GiB) exists in the abliterated line.
- Notes (vision_speech_image_models.md): "Z-Image ~12 GB" estimated at BF16; the published Tongyi-MAI/Z-Image-Turbo transformer is stored as F32 at 24.620 GB (text encoder 8.045 GB BF16, repo total 32.900 GB); a BF16 copy would be about 12.3 GB, so the report's "within 16 GB" stands only after casting.

### cards-licences

- Report: Baichuan-M3-235B is 'served via SGLang or vLLM with an official GPTQ-INT4 and no GGUF mentioned' and 'a community GGUF is plausible but unverified'. Correction: Baichuan publishes an official GGUF, baichuan-inc/Baichuan-M3-235B-Q4_K_M-GGUF (10 shards, 143,782,829,376 bytes = 143.78 GB = 133.91 GiB, lastModified 2026-02-09), whose card says 'you can use llama.cpp or ollama'; official FP8 and GPTQ-INT4 repos also exist. The 44.4 Hard / 42 GPT-5.2-High / 65.1 Total / 3.5% figures are confirmed from the card's image (vendor-run).
- Report: GLM Legal 'is almost certainly a GLM-5-class post-train (744B or 320B-Flash based)' with licence and GGUF 'unobtainable'. Correction: z.ai's blog states it is built on GLM-5.3-Flash (320B total / 18B active, MIT base); no Hugging Face repository exists under zai-org, z.ai offers only a gated API beta ('Join the GLM-Legal Beta / Apply for API access'), and Artificial Lawyer reports it is 'not intended, at least not yet, to be an open weights model'. Vals.ai nevertheless labels it 'open-weight' and lists 86.24% (#14 of 150) on LegalBench; the report's 'top open-weight model' phrasing and the '88.6% closed cluster' were not found in the page text, so that ranking claim remains unverified.
- Report: 'huihui-ai is the only producer with a near-complete GGUF line across every candidate family (gpt-oss-120b, ...)'. Correction: huihui-ai's gpt-oss-120b abliteration repos (BF16-abliterated, BF16-abliterated-v2, mxfp4-abliterated, mxfp4-abliterated-v2) now return HTTP 401 (not found or private) and are absent from the huihui-ai author listing, which shows only gpt-oss-20b abliterations; the node's noctrex MXFP4_MOE file still exists but its stated base card cannot be read.
- Report: 'HiDream-I1 (17B, MIT)'. Correction: MIT covers only the Transformer; the bundled meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct text encoder is under the Llama 3.1 Community License (VAE and T5 encoders Apache 2.0).
- Report: Flash-Next's 'licence ... still being written down' and the follow-up list's 'licences of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next ... unverified'. Resolved: Qwen Community License 1.0 (not Apache): commercial use and derivatives permitted, but a 'Model as a Service' or 'AI Work Assistant' business needs a separate licence for any commercial use, and products above 100M MAU or US$20M monthly revenue must display the model name.
- Report: 'Nemotron Puzzle ... licence ... unverified'. Resolved: OpenMDW-1.1 ('ready for commercial use'), released 2026-07-06, 75.3B / 9.3B active, derived from Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B.
- Report: Gemma 4 'reported Apache 2.0 licensing, but ... needs the official card to confirm'. Resolved: the HF cards (gemma-4-31B-it, gemma-4-26B-A4B-it, gemma-4-31B) carry license: apache-2.0, are not gated, and link to ai.google.dev/gemma/docs/gemma_4_license, which reproduces the Apache 2.0 text; neither incorporates the Gemma Terms or the Prohibited Use Policy (still published, last updated 2024-08-05).
- Report: 'Qwen3-Coder-Next ... licence unverified'. Partly resolved: card front matter says apache-2.0, but the LICENSE file it links to does not exist in the repo (404).
- Minor: GLM-OCR's HF card states 94.62 on OmniDocBench V1.5, while the report's 95.22 is the v1.6 leaderboard value; Qwen3-ASR's '52 languages' is '52 languages and dialects' (30 languages + 22 Chinese dialects); Qwen-Image-2512's card does not state the 20B parameter count the report gives (Apache 2.0 confirmed).

### server-paths

- Follow-up section: "the pinned commit b29c606's date must be confirmed against the 14 September 2026 version bump, because the two notes disagree" -> confirmed: b29c606e28a01b1bc8c1351026a0fa6e616bf6c4 is the "llama.cpp : bump version to 0.4.1 (#28900)" commit dated 2026-09-14T17:01:05+03:00 (git log at tag v0.4.1; release page "released this 14 Sep 18:27"). The companion note's "roughly mid-June 2026" inference is wrong. Consequences: Qwen3.8-Flash-Next (#27742, 27 Aug; arch qwen4exp present in the v0.4.1 registry), Qwen3-TTS (#26254, 4 Aug), Nemotron Nano MTP (#26725, 10 Aug) and Nemotron Puzzle (#25444, 3 Sep; NemotronHPuzzleForCausalLM registered in v0.4.1 conversion/nemotron.py) are inside the pin.
- Core seats, Nemotron 3 Super: "Its MTP support merged inside the pin on 10 August 2026" and the overview's "Nemotron MTP (10 August)" / table "MTP support inside the pin" -> contradicted. PR #26725 "adds MTP support for the Nemotron Nano model"; Nemotron 3 Super MTP required PR #29018 "model : extend Nemotron MTP support", merged 2026-09-17, three days after the pin, because "Super 3 has an additional latent compression step, which is not present in other Nemotron models" and an MTP export previously failed to load ("wrong number of tensors; expected 781, got 779"). At v0.4.1 --spec-type draft-mtp is not available for Nemotron 3 Super; the ordinary non-MTP Q8_0 file is unaffected.
- Vision engine: the caveat "DeepStack was temporarily removed" and the follow-up item "Qwen3.5-122B's document quality with the mmproj and without DeepStack" -> the released Qwen/Qwen3.5-122B-A10B config.json has vision_config "deepstack_visual_indexes": [], i.e. the model has no DeepStack layers; the PR #19468 maintainer note says the removal was "confirmed by transformers implementation"; no restoration PR exists (title and body searches, 2026-10-14). The "without DeepStack" qualifier describes no deficit and should be dropped.
- Medicine, Baichuan-M3-235B: "served via SGLang or vLLM with an official GPTQ-INT4 and no GGUF mentioned" and "a community GGUF is plausible but unverified" -> an official baichuan-inc/Baichuan-M3-235B-Q4_K_M-GGUF exists (10 shards, 143,782,829,376 B = 143.8 GB = 133.9 GiB; card: deploy with llama.cpp or ollama), plus mradermacher static and imatrix sets, DevQuasar and spicyneuron GGUFs; config.json architectures ["Qwen3MoeForCausalLM"] (model_type qwen3_moe) is registered in the v0.4.1 converter. Also "at roughly 130 to 144 GB the model fits only at Q4" -> mradermacher Q5_K_S is 161.9 GB = 150.8 GiB and Q5_K_M 166.8 GB = 155.4 GiB, both under the 164 GiB engine budget before KV; Q6_K (179.8 GiB) and Q8_0 (232.8 GiB) do not fit.
- Retrieval: "confirmation that the llama-server embedding and reranking endpoints accept these architectures, which the notes do not establish" -> established for the pin: both use arch qwen3; Qwen's official Qwen3-Embedding-8B GGUF carries pooling_type = 3 (LAST) and Qwen's card prescribes "--embedding --pooling last -ub 8192"; rerank requires --reranking (or --embedding --pooling rank) and a GGUF converted with the reranker head (pooling RANK, classifier labels yes/no, "rerank" chat template), which the v0.4.1 converter writes for Qwen3-Reranker (PR #15824, 2025-09-25). Two caveats the report lacks: (a) at v0.4.1 a rerank query+document longer than n_ubatch is rejected ("input (N tokens) is too large to process. increase the physical batch size"); the fix (PR #28876) merged 2026-09-27, after the pin, so -ub must be sized to the longest pair; (b) the only official reranker GGUF is ggml-org/Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B-Q8_0-GGUF; no Qwen-authored reranker GGUF exists, and the mradermacher Qwen3-Reranker-8B Q8_0 file has no pooling/classifier/rerank metadata (converted without the head), so the 4B/8B rerankers must be converted locally.
- Speech, Qwen3-TTS: "gained llama.cpp support on 4 August 2026, inside the pin, which makes the document's 'test after core build' watch-list entry executable" -> true only for the llama-tts CLI; no llama-server TTS endpoint exists at the pin or on master (PR #26603 "server : add POST /tts endpoint for TTS models" open since 2026-08-04). The Vulkan GET_ROWS fix Qwen3-TTS needed (#28253) merged 2026-09-07, inside the pin; CustomVoice conversion fix #29179 (2026-10-07) is outside it.
- Speech, Qwen3-ASR: the report's "official ggml-org GGUF in llama.cpp's supported list" is confirmed at v0.4.1 (docs/multimodal.md); add that the pinned server offers both /v1/chat/completions input_audio and an OpenAI-compatible /v1/audio/transcriptions route (PR #21863, 2026-04-14), undocumented in the v0.4.1 README.
- Measured-decode section, "Native MTP speculative decoding is the one free speed-up" and the note that DeepSeek V4 MTP coverage was only inferred from community runs -> primary record: PR #25784 "DeepseekV4 MTP + DSpark" merged 2026-08-02 (inside the pin) but states "Deepseek did not ship MTP with the latest deepseek models (0731). Only use DSpark!"; MTP applies to the original V4 Flash checkpoint only. The companion note's claim that the DeepSeek MTP/DSpark PRs "were closed without merging" is superseded by #25784.
- Flash-Next: the report's "an MTP head that was 'still WIP' at merge" is consistent with the pin: src/models/qwen4exp.cpp at v0.4.1 has no MTP graph; "Qwen4Exp: add MTP" (#29761) merged 2026-10-01, so every Flash-Next MTP throughput figure in the notes comes from post-pin builds.
- Strix Halo backend note: issue #25992 was closed "not planned" by the stale bot on 2026-10-09 without a linked fix, but the trigger commit c7d8722 was reverted by #28604 on 2026-09-08, inside the pin (v0.4.1 ggml-cuda.cu line 307 forces integrated = false), so a HIP build of the pinned tag does not carry that regression; #20732 (Nemotron 3 Super 20K-prompt memory fault on Vulkan, build b8414) closed "not planned" on 2026-05-03 with no fix and no maintainer reproduction.

### quality-leaderboards

- Report line 3: "almost no candidate carries an independent benchmark score on a current index" — contradicted. Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index v4.3.2 (read 2026-10-14) scores 15 of the 16 candidates (all but Baichuan-M3): GLM-5.3-Flash 42, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 40, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 39, MiMo-V2.6-Flash 38, DeepSeek V4 Flash 0731 34, Qwen3.8 27B 34, Hy3 25, Step 3.7 Flash 19, Qwen3.5 122B A10B 16, Gemma 4 31B 15, Mistral Medium 3.5 14, Nemotron 3 Super 13, gpt-oss-120b 12, Mistral Small 4 11, Qwen3 Coder Next 9. LMArena text (publish 2026-10-13) lists 11 of them; Vals LegalBench 6; EQ-Bench 5.
- Report line 114 (follow-up section): "no independent score exists on a current index for Qwen3.5-122B-A10B, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, GLM-5.3-Flash, Step-3.7-Flash, Nemotron 3 Super or Baichuan-M3" — contradicted for every model except Baichuan-M3: AA v4.3.2 gives 16, 40, 42, 19 and 13 respectively; LMArena text gives qwen3.5-122b-a10b 1417.4, glm-5.3-flash 1471.0, nvidia-nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b 1377.6; LMArena WebDev gives qwen3.8-flash-next 1633.2 (rank 18). Baichuan-M3 remains absent from AA, LMArena, Vals and EQ-Bench.
- Report line 82: "Against it [Qwen3.8-Flash-Next] stand four unknowns: no independent score" — contradicted: AA v4.3.2 40 (GPQA 92.3, HLE 38.0, TB4.0 25.3), LMArena WebDev 1633.2, legacy LiveBench 2026-06-25 row qwen3.8-flash-next (global mean 76.2, computed). Also AA's model metadata lists its licence as "Qwen Community License 1.0" and its size as 180B total / 6B active, versus the report's "125B-A6B" (line 1) — the parameter count should be re-checked.
- Report line 72: "Gemma 4 31B (MMMU-Pro 76.9, Arena 1451)" — the Arena figure is the June 2026 tech-report snapshot; LMArena text as of 2026-10-13 is 1442.8 [1435.4-1450.2] (style-control 1452.1), and AA's independent MMMU-Pro for Gemma 4 31B (Reasoning) is 73.4, below the vendor 76.9.
- Report line 82/84 ordering claims hold but with much smaller margins on the current index: on v4.3.2 Gemma 4 31B 15 and Nemotron 3 Super 13 versus gpt-oss-120b 12 (the report cites 39.2 / 36.0 / 33.3 from an older index version); LMArena text also orders them nemotron-3-super 1377.6 > gpt-oss-120b 1365.4. Not a contradiction, but the 'older index' numbers should be replaced by v4.3.2 values.
- Confirmed, not contradicted: 'AA ~34' for Qwen3.8-27B (exactly 34, Xhigh); gpt-oss-120b 12 on v4.3.2 and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 39; gpt-oss HealthBench 57.6 / Hard 30.0 (card Table 3) and Baichuan-M3 65.1 / 44.4 (card chart); Qwen3.5-397B-A17B tau2 84.43 / 97.81 / 9.79; Devstral (2512) 53.8 on swebench.com; Kokoro ~1064 Elo (AA 1063); Qwen3-ASR-1.7B below whisper-large-v3 on every Open ASR English set.

### quant-tables

- Line 21 and line 86 (PR #21038): the report says the rotation is explicitly "not applied to MLA models"; the PR body's exact words are "Note: MLA is not supported". The "kept off for MLA models ... upstream does not rotate MLA either" wording comes from a downstream backport comment on the PR page (wanghqc, 2 Oct 2026), not from ggerganov's description.
- Line 84 (RefusalBench): "a 0.4% refusal rate on 141 benign biology research prompts" is mis-scoped. RefusalBench's 141 prompts span three tiers (47 bundles x benign/borderline/dual-use); Nemotron 3 Super's 0.4% is its benign-tier strict refusal rate, with 0.9% borderline, 6.8% dual-use and 2.7% overall, and it refused 89.3% (67/75) of the should-refuse control prompts, i.e. it is calibrated rather than merely compliant. Kimi K2.6's 91.5% / 97.0% / 95.3% (94.6% overall) is correctly described. Nemotron was a v1.1 sensitivity-check addition adjudicated by a separate sub-council and is excluded from the paper's 18-model statistics. Also for the Estate comparison: GLM-5 0.4% benign / 3.3% overall; DeepSeek V3.2 0.0% / 0.6%; DeepSeek R1 0.4% / 0.3%; Mistral Large 3 0.4% / 0.4%.
- Line 19 (Token Arena): the quoted phrase should read "even when MMLU-style smoke tests show no gap", not "even where". The -5.5 MATH-500 and -9.0 AIME-25 deltas are confirmed (Table 3, n=6 FP8/Turbo vs n=13 BF16, fidelity 92.1 vs 99.7); note the paper's Table 2 caption cites a 12.5-point AIME-2025 gap while its own row gives 41.5-51.0 (9.5 points).
- Line 30 (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B sizes): the Unsloth repo today gives UD-Q4_K_XL 77.0 GB (71.7 GiB), not 78.6 GB; UD-Q6_K_XL 112.4 GB = 104.7 GiB, not 106 GiB (106 GB is Unsloth's "6-bit" total-memory requirement, not the file); Q8_0 129.9 GB (121.0 GiB) matches "130 GB". The Mar 5 2026 re-quant changed file sizes, which explains the older community figures.
- Line 37 and line 86 (Qwen3.5-397B): UD-IQ3_XXS 140.3 GB (130.7 GiB) is confirmed from the repo, and "3-bit: fits on 192GB RAM systems" is now primary. The report's "3-bit only" is correct, but the Unsloth page's "UD-Q4_K_XL is ~214GB on disk" conflicts with the repo's UD-Q4_K_XL folder (245.3 GB); UD-IQ4_XS is 189.7 GB.
- Line 108 (abliterated engine): "0.5 is right at the threshold Heretic itself flags as 'may indicate significant damage'" cannot be traced to Heretic: that phrase is absent from the Heretic README and source as of 14 Oct 2026; only the Ollama repackager says it. The kldzj figures (0.53 / 22/100 for v2, 0.92 for v1, 19/100) are confirmed primary. The Heretic README's own reference points are gemma-3-12b-it: heretic 0.16, huihui 0.45, mlabonne 1.04.
- Lines 19, 28, 68, 86 (DeepSeek V4 Flash): all Unsloth figures are now primary (UD-Q8_K_XL 161.9 GB bit-exact on all 1,328 tensors, KLD ~0, 100% same top token; UD-Q4_K_XL 155.1 GB, KLD 0.0102, 96.28% same top token, 97.46% bit-exact weights, non-expert 4% to Q8_0). The 6.8 GB difference is 6,774,374,400 bytes (6.77 GB / 6.31 GiB) and 161.9 GB = 150.8 GiB, both confirmed. One nuance the report omits: Unsloth says Q8_0/F16 on some non-expert tensors is itself not lossless under DeepSeek's QAT, so the Q8_K_XL keeps them in BF16; Q4_K_XL's 0.0102 KLD comes from exactly that downcast.
- Line 42 (Gemma 4 31B): the QAT UD-Q4_K_XL 17.3 GB figure is confirmed (17.29 GB), with mean KLD 0.01403 and top-1 96.67% vs naive Q4_0 0.09349 / 87.91%; the "higher precision is worse" finding is primary. The report's Q8_0 "≈33 GB" estimate for the non-QAT 31B is not contradicted (Unsloth lists 8-bit at 34-38 GB total memory).
- Line 3 and the evidence-marking paragraph: the Unsloth Qwen3.8 page names the family as 27B, 2.4T-A95B and Max, with Flash-Next on a separate page; the Flash-Next GGUF card carries licence "other" / license_name "qwen-community-1.0" (not Apache), which bears on the follow-up list's open licence question. The Flash-Next KLD ladder (UD-Q4_K_XL 111.3 GB KLD 0.0469, top-1 92.3%; UD-Q6_K_XL 169.2 GB KLD 0.0271, top-1 94.1%; Q8_0 192.4 GB) is now available and contradicts the follow-up list's statement that no Flash-Next rung above IQ4_XS has a measured size.
- Research note (quantization_precision.md), not the report: the "unlabeled" 19.2 / 20.7 / 5.894 / 2.877 figures are Old GB / New GB / Old Max KLD / New Max KLD for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B UD-Q4_K_XL (Mar 5 2026 update), not KLD per se; and the Unsloth Dynamic 2.0 KLD/MMLU tables are Gemma 3 27B only (Llama 4 Scout is image-only), on a page now titled "Unsloth Dynamic 3.0 GGUFs".

### hardware-192

- Report (Measured decode section): "Every speed figure below comes from 128 GB Ryzen AI Max+ 395 systems" and (budget section) "the only 192 GB measurement found anywhere is a GLM-5.3-Flash GGUF ... 17.4 tok/s". Contradicted: Framework's 2026-09-30 blog (community mirror topic 85191) and local-llm-benchmarks.dev carry 15 Gorgon Halo (PRO 495) runs dated 2026-09-23 to 09-30: DeepSeek V4.1 Flash Q2 via DwarfStar 14.0-14.8 tok/s (16.3 with DSpark), MiMo V2.6 Flash RL MXFP4 via llama.cpp ROCm 10.0 18.3 tok/s (16.2 at 64K), GLM-5.3 Flash Q2 11.9 tok/s, Qwen3.8 Flash Next Q2 23-25 tok/s, DeepSeek V4 Flash IQ2XXS 15 tok/s. None covers the roster's four engines at their planned quants, so the report's conclusion that V10 must measure them stands.
- Report: "the PRO 495's LPDDR5X-8533 raises bandwidth from 256 to about 273 GB/s, a 6.6% ceiling lift and nothing more (VideoCardz, excerpt)". Framework (primary) confirms 273 GB/s and "about 6.7 percent" but also a maximum GPU clock rise from 2.9 to 3.0 GHz, and HP's 192 GB ZBook Ultra G3a runs LPDDR5X at 8000 MT/s, not 8533, so the uplift is vendor-dependent. AMD's product page lists LPDDR5x-8533 and 256-bit but no bandwidth figure; the "up to 160 GB dedicated graphics memory" statement is AMD footnote GRHP-01 (as of 2026-05-11).
- Report: "DeepSeek V4.1 Flash exists ... but is a 552B model whose smallest usable GGUF is 246 GiB ... so it is unusable here". Framework's blog shows it running on one 192 GB Gorgon Halo with antirez's calibrated Q2 file in DwarfStar ("552B main + 196B Engram", "About 163GB of main weights loaded in memory; about 366GB for the complete file", Engram tables left on disk) at 14 tok/s with 64K-context curves. The statement holds only for the llama.cpp v0.4.1 pin, not for the hardware.
- Report: "the issue was auto-closed 'not planned' on 9 October 2026 with AMD's fix still unmerged". Partly contradicted: the bisected cause (c7d87229, PR #24233 restoring prop.integrated on HIP) was reverted by PR #28604, merged 2026-09-08 and listed in the v0.4.1 release changelog, so the pinned build already carries the revert. What remains unmerged is the UMA scheduler rework: #25863 (author Victor-Loos, closed unmerged 2026-09-14), #27311 (draft since 2026-09-16) and AMD's mapatel-amd #29450 (draft since 2026-09-26).
- Report: "its card suggests a TTM setting that 'gives the GPU 176 GiB'" (excerpt) is confirmed primary; add that the card reports mem_info_gtt_total about 189,000,000,000 bytes, keeps the BIOS carve-out at minimum, measured decode 17.4 tok/s at depth 0 but 15.0 at 32K context (prefill 256.4 then 95.9 t/s), on ROCmFPX main (Vulkan) with plain llama.cpp master giving "the same decode speed", on Ubuntu 26.04 / kernel 7.0 / Mesa 26.0.8 RADV; the file is 154,119,930,880 bytes (154.1 GB / 143.5 GiB) in 4 shards and the repo dates from 2026-10-02.
- Research-note claim (not in the report but feeding it) that AMD's listing gives "273 GB/s bandwidth" and "up to 160 GB can be dedicated to graphics memory": the AMD product page has neither; both figures come from the AMD blog footnote (160 GB) and Framework (273 GB/s).
- ROCm status previously marked "could not be read": ROCm 10.0.0 (2026-08-26) and 10.1.0 (2026-10-05) both list "AMD Ryzen AI Max+ PRO 495 (Radeon 8065S) (gfx1151)" as supported, with Ubuntu 26.04 (10.0.0) and Ubuntu 26.04.1 kernel GA 7.0 (10.1.0) as supported Radeon/Ryzen OS rows, and AMD's RDNA3.5 page marks "Ubuntu 26.04 Generic" as a stable combination for 10.0.0/10.1.0.
